FAQ

What's the highest scoring 8-letter word? ZYZZYVAS at 44 Scrabble points.

How many 8-letter words are there? 28420 words are valid in standard word-game dictionaries.

Back to WordCrack

Are these words valid in Scrabble and Words With Friends? Yes — this list uses a standard tournament word dictionary accepted by both games.
